14 template<typename Func> struct constant_t;
15
16 template<template<typename> typename Func, typename Opts> struct constant_t<Func<Opts>> : Func<Opts>
17 {
18 using base_callable = Func<Opts>;
19
20 template<typename T>
21 EVE_FORCEINLINE constexpr auto operator[](T t) const
22 requires(requires(base_callable const& b) { b[t]; })
23 {
24 auto new_traits = base_callable::base::operator[](t);
25 return constant_t<Func<decltype(new_traits)>>{new_traits};
26 }
27
28 template<concepts::real T>
29 43433 EVE_FORCEINLINE constexpr T operator()(as<T> const& v) const
30 requires(requires(base_callable const& b) { b(v); })
31 {
32 43433 return base_callable::operator()(v);
33 }
